local Players = game:GetService("Players")

-- Parse "userid: Country emoji Nx" lines -> { [userId] = "Country" }
local watchlist = {}
for line in DATA:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
    line = line:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
    if line ~= "" then
        local uid, country = line:match("^(%d+)%s*:%s*(.+)$")
        if uid then
            -- strip trailing "Nx" counter and any trailing whitespace
            country = country:gsub("%s*%d+x%s*$", "")
            country = country:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            watchlist[tonumber(uid)] = country
        end
    end
end

print("[CountryWatcher] Loaded " .. (function()
    local n = 0; for _ in pairs(watchlist) do n = n + 1 end return n
end)() .. " tracked players.")

-- ===== GET PLAYER'S COUNTRY TAG (from the game's overhead UI) =====
local function getCountryTag(player)
    -- Method 1: StringValue on the player object
    local tag = player:FindFirstChild("Country") or player:FindFirstChild("CountryTag")
    if tag and tag:IsA("StringValue") and tag.Value ~= "" then
        return tag.Value
    end

    -- Method 2: BillboardGui on the character (the tag above their head)
    local char = player.Character
    if char then
        for _, part in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                for _, bb in pairs(part:GetChildren()) do
                    if bb:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                        for _, label in pairs(bb:GetDescendants()) do
                            if label:IsA("TextLabel") and label.Text then
                                local t = label.Text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
                                if #t >= 2 and #t <= 3 and t:upper() == t and t:match("^%u%u") then
                                    return t
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- Method 3: PlayerGui fallback
    local pg = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if pg then
        for _, gui in pairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
            if (gui:IsA("TextLabel") or gui:IsA("TextButton")) and gui.Text then
                local t = gui.Text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
                if #t >= 2 and #t <= 3 and t:upper() == t and t:match("^%u%u") then
                    return t
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return nil
end

-- ===== OVERHEAD LABEL =====
local function createLabel(player)
    if player == Players.LocalPlayer then return end
    local trackedCountry = watchlist[player.UserId]
    if not trackedCountry then return end -- only label people on your list

    local holder = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    holder.Name = "CountryWatcherTag"
    holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 30)
    holder.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
    holder.AlwaysOnTop = true
    holder.MaxDistance = 1000

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = "Label"
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = trackedCountry .. " (loading tag...)"
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
    label.TextScaled = true
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.Parent = holder

    local function attach(char)
        local head = char:WaitForChild("Head", 10) or char:WaitForChildWhichIsA("BasePart", 10)
        if head then holder.Parent = head end
    end

    if player.Character then attach(player.Character) end
    player.CharacterAdded:Connect(attach)

    -- live update: verify the game's own tag matches your saved country
    task.spawn(function()
        while holder and holder.Parent and player.Parent do
            local country = getCountryTag(player)
            if country then
                label.Text = trackedCountry .. "  |  " .. country
                label.TextColor3 = (country:upper() == trackedCountry:upper())
                    and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
                    or Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)
            else
                label.Text = trackedCountry .. " (?)" -- tag not loaded yet
            end
            task.wait(1)
        end
        if holder then holder:Destroy() end
    end)
end

-- ===== ATTACH TO EVERYONE + LIVE JOIN/LEAVE =====
for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    task.spawn(createLabel, player)
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
    task.wait(1)
    createLabel(player)
end)

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
    if watchlist[player.UserId] then
        print("[CountryWatcher] TRACKED player left: " .. player.Name .. " (" .. watchlist[player.UserId] .. ")")
    end
end)

print("[CountryWatcher] Running — labels update every second.")
